# zk.de forum: full sweep of all public threads

Ticket: https://github.com/theaustinschool/game-design-map/issues/16
Scope: every discussion visible to logged-out readers on forum.zettelkasten.de, all categories, every non-staff post read in full.
Swept: 2026-11-04

| 16-441 | Give notes descriptive file names so they can be found by keyword search. | here is a short note that has a descriptive file name that could be easily fuzzy searched based on keywords | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/3040#Comment_3040 | monk | 2019-07-05 | title | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 16-442 | Writing a note helps solidify the memory itself. | A large part of why I even take the notes is to help solidify the memory so I don't have to search it again. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/3040#Comment_3040 | monk | 2019-07-05 | process | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 16-443 | Keeping a whole Zettelkasten in one huge file fails because software chokes on very large files. | some pieces of software are going to choke on Extremely Large Files | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/3050#Comment_3050 | mediapathic | 2019-07-09 | tooling,failure-mode | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |

| 16-445 | Processing inbox entries into notes as a separate step adds thinking time that helps internalize understanding. | I find that it adds a layer of taking the time to think about an entry which helps me with internalizing understanding. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/3050#Comment_3050 | mediapathic | 2019-07-09 | inbox,process | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 16-446 | Use UIDs for links: easy to set up, less clutter than full titles, and titles can change without breaking links. | What I find very convenient about using UIDs is that links are very easy to set up, they don't clutter the zettel like a complete title would and you can change zettel titles without destroying links to the corresponding zettels. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5330#Comment_5330 | Vinho | 2020-04-15 | id,linking | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |

## Conflicts noticed

- Skip internal note links in favor of strict atomicity (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/285#Comment_285) vs Every note pointing onward to related notes makes retrieval and branching work (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/304#Comment_304)
- Think a minute before each note, keep minute-resolution IDs (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/607#Comment_607) vs Notes get created in rapid succession, so add seconds to IDs (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/606#Comment_606)
- Avoid IDs of different lengths (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/609#Comment_609) vs Suffix colliding IDs with .1, .2 (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/681#Comment_681)
- Link the source right in the note text (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/289#Comment_289) vs Keep references hidden in a comment / metadata so text stays undisturbed (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/627#Comment_627)
- knowledge must be verified in reality (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/13808#Comment_13808) vs no epistemic requirement to prove recorded understanding (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/13810#Comment_13810)
- key-value tags may be a beginner's mistake (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/485#Comment_485) vs use status key-value tags like #subs:out (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/87/techniques-for-tag-heirarchies)
- summarizing others' writing in notes is of doubtful value (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/81/in-which-i-ask-myself-does-building-a-second-brain-distract-from-doing-hard-work-in-my-real-brain) vs mark text to build a narrativised summary turned into a zettel (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/494#Comment_494)
- numeric/timestamp IDs in filenames are a big plus (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/1534#Comment_1534) vs date-time-first filenames confuse human-readable organization (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/2405#Comment_2405)
- Only add a note when you know how to link it into the web (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/691#Comment_691) vs overthinking inclusion risks no note; you can't predict connections (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/739#Comment_739)
- ZK suits a graph, not project management, which suits hierarchy (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/761#Comment_761) vs project management should use ZK-like network structure (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/760#Comment_760)
- Use buffer notes vs just tag the notes instead (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/849#Comment_849) vs buffer note as a visible cushion at the top of the archive (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/3272#Comment_3272)
- two-digit-year IDs fine, fixable later by macro (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/1092#Comment_1092) vs use four-digit years to avoid rollover disaster (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/1110#Comment_1110)
- links should be UID only so titles can change (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/2516#Comment_2516) vs title-based links are self-documenting (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/146/feature-discussion-linking-to-notes-using-uids-inside-the-notes)
- prefix filenames with subject, no UID (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/921#Comment_921) vs give zettels surrogate-key IDs and link by ID (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/781#Comment_781)
- per-note timestamp IDs are cumbersome, ID per topic (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/1832#Comment_1832) vs surrogate-key ID per zettel (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/781#Comment_781)
- one pass cannot extract a text's value, re-read as needed (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/1156#Comment_1156) vs fully process material rather than keep it unprocessed (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/1067#Comment_1067)
- argonsnorts titles notes with colons (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/1448#Comment_1448) vs rene avoid colons in titles for portability (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/1449#Comment_1449)
- Keep notes in plain text because nothing is more portable (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/2563#Comment_2563) vs prefer high-fidelity zettels with media over plain-text 'prepper mentality' (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/2641#Comment_2641)
- Put full reference at the bottom of each content zettel (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/2596#Comment_2596) vs citekey plus .bib file is enough, no bibliography data in the note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/2697#Comment_2697)
- A zettel is written with atomicity in mind (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9290#Comment_9290) vs one concept need not be expressed by a single note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9243#Comment_9243)
- Do not create orphans, start every note as a link from another (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/2529#Comment_2529) vs add links only when a note is used in producing a paper (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/2530#Comment_2530)
- link by UIDs so titles can change without breaking links (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5330#Comment_5330) vs link by full titles, fix renames via find and replace (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5337#Comment_5337)
- fuzzy full-text search can replace links (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/3040#Comment_3040) vs connect your thoughts, linking is the core advantage (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/625/the-zettelkasten-as-a-lattice-of-thought-strings)

## Coverage log

Method for every row: Vanilla forum API v2 on forum.zettelkasten.de, anonymous, throttled to one request per 1.5 s. Enumeration: `GET /api/v2/discussions?limit=100&page=1..31&sort=discussionID&expand=insertUser` (opening posts in full). Replies: `GET /api/v2/comments?discussionID={id}&limit=100&page=N&expand=insertUser` until a short page. Every thread below was read in full; status `read-no-claims` means every post was read and none made a claim about note quality or construction. Staff posts (Sascha, ctietze; ticket #7) and the `System` bot were read for context only. Claims already in tickets #7 or #8 were not re-added; the note column names prior ids where they apply.

## Known gaps

- Sweep stopped early: the user wrapped up gathering on 2026-11-04. 2938 of 3057 enumerated discussions were not reached; each has a `failed` coverage row with the note "not reached: wrapped up per user 2026-11-04".
- Enumeration: the anonymous listing (`GET /api/v2/discussions?sort=discussionID`, 31 pages) returned 3057 unique discussions with ids 7 to 3581, while its `x-app-page-result-count` header said 3025 and the sum of `countDiscussions` in `GET /api/v2/categories` was 2992. The listing includes categories 14 (88 threads) and 22 (16 threads), which `/api/v2/categories` does not list. The planned per-id probe of the roughly 518 ids missing from the listing (to separate login-gated from deleted threads) was not run before the wrap-up.
- Posters who formatted their own words as a blockquote (with no attribution) could not be quoted until a reader rule change partway through the run; shards s001-s018 were read under the stricter rule, so such passages there may be under-recorded (one known case: Edmund, https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/22802#Comment_22802).
- Login-gated areas (members-only categories, The Perfect Zettel course) are out of scope for this ticket and were not swept.
- Staff posts (Sascha, ctietze) are ticket #7's and are not recorded here.
- PARTIAL: sweep in progress; more reading shards are still to be merged.
